miṉṉañcal: 
 tāḷ : 16.0 ki. mēplittō,  aḷavu : 1/8 temmi 
 eḻuttu : 11.5 puḷḷi,  pakkam : 344
kaṭṭamippu: iyalpu  paṭikaḷ : 1000 

  accu : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ,  
āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi - 600 006. 


kaṭappāṭṭu uri
tamiḻ iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻippaṭṭa oli, vari, vaṭivukaḷiyum iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal, meyyiyal, vāḻviyal, paṭippiyal eṉpavaṟṟi aṭimaṉiyākavum koṇṭa moḻi.
ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum mūla muḻakkatti muḻuvatākak koṇṭatu nammoḻi eṉpati ōrāṟṟāṉ viḷakkuvatāka amintatu ik kaḷañciyam.
āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉpavi illāmal, veḷippaṭa evarum aṟintu koṇṭuḷḷa vaḻakku, ceyyuḷ eṉpavi koṇṭē viḷakkuvatu ikkaḷañciyam.
itaṉi, ūṉṟiyum virumpiyum kaṟpār tāmum ivvāyvu ceyya viyalum eṉpatit tūṇṭa valla āyvum itu! ātalāl, paṭippāḷiyip paṭippāḷi ākkavallatu eṉpati eṉ paṭṭaṟivut teḷivākak kaṇṭu varukiṟēṉ! eḷimiyāy - āḻnta nuṇukkaṅkaḷi - ōraḷavu kaṟṟārum, koḷḷa vaḻikāṭṭum `kiviḷakku aṉṉatu itu.
itaṉi mutal - iṭi - niṟivu eṉṉum mūṉṟu nili kaḷilum uṟṟa peṟalarum utavunarāka iruntu muṟṟuvittatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ ulakap payaṉpāṭṭukkum vaḻiyamittut tanta `kalaṅkari viḷakkam cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tiruttaku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷē āvar. ātalāl, avarkaḷi neñcārap pōṟṟuvatu eṉ talikkaṭaṉām!
tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvaṉa vaḻiyē ippaṇi ceyya vāytta aḷavil coṟṟiraṭṭiyum meyppup pārttuma utaviya peruntaku kēṇmiyum uḻuvalaṉpum uṭiya muṉivar peruntaki ku. tirumāṟaṉār avarkaḷukkum em eḻuttuppaṇiyit tam paṇiyāk koṇṭu toṭarntu ceytuvarum toyvilāt toṇṭar meyppup pārttalil vallār muṉivar pi. tamiḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
eṉ eḻuttip paṭiyeṭuttum, koṅku vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷit tokuttum utaviya pulavar kalimaṇiyār avarkaḷukkum, ippaṇiyil illi eṉiṉum uḻuva laṉpālum urimip pēṟṟālum eṉ eḻuttukaḷip paṭiyeṭuttu utaviya tirukkuṟaḷ cemmal perumpulavar mu. paṭikkarāmu avarkaḷukkum perunaṉṟiyum vāḻttum uṭiyēṉ.
ittoki aṉittiyum kaṇiṉippaṭuttiyum, meyppup pārttum, varavu celavu kaṇakkit takka vakit taṇikkikku uriyaviyākac ceytum, paṇiniṟivi oppaṭittum perunaṉṟāṟṟiya kavaṉakac cemmal muṉivar kali. ceḻiyaṉār aruntoṇṭip pārāṭṭi naṉṟi perituṭiyēṉ.
paṇi niṟivuppayaṉ, tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakap payaṉpāṭu ātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉē! illākkāl ceyta paṇiyāl āvateṉṉa? ceyyāmi oppatu tāṉē! avvāṟu ākāmal tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷuṭaṉ, ayalakat tamiḻp perumakkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉum tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷilum mēmpaṭṭa ārvalarkaḷākak kuviyattu, amerikkā, kaṉaṭā, maliyakam, ciṅkapuri, īḻam mutalāka vāḻvār ārvat taḷirppum tūṇṭalum muṉṉiṟkum pēṟu viyappukkum perunaṉṟikkum uriyatām;
ipperumakkaḷ aṉivar uḷḷamum ōruruvāka vāyttu, karuvi nūṟpaṇiyit toki tokiyāka vakippaṭutti vāṉ peruntoṇ ṭākac ceytalē em piṟavik kaṭaṉ eṉak koṇṭa tamiḻp pōrāḷi tirumiku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar eṉpati nilināṭṭiyatip pōṟṟuvataṉṟi eṉ colvēṉ?
kaḷañciyam kaṇiṉippaṭuttiya aḷavil niṟivuṟumā? pattut tokutikaḷiyum orumuṟikku mummuṟiyāy muḻutuṟa meyppup pārttal, eḷimip pāṭā? kaṇpuri vaḷarntālum iṭittaṭi nērntālum ittoṇṭu tamiḻaṉṉi taḷirkkum toṇṭu eṉṟu imip poḻutum cōrātu kaṭaṉāṟṟiya kaṭappāṭṭāḷar meyppuccemmal muṉivar pi. tamiḻakaṉārkkup peruṅkaṭappāṭu uṭiyēṉ; patippuc cumiyi ēṟṟami eṉ cumi ēṟṟamiyām!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nalaṉē! 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



kaḷañciyam
vaḷamāṉa vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻum nilakkiḻār perunilakkiḻār ākiya uḻavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ uṭpakutiyil kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ amittiruppar. kaḷañciyam irukkum vīṭu eṉṟāl vaḷamikka uḻavarvīṭu eṉpatu poruḷ.
kaḷattil varum tavacaṅkaḷ payaṟu vakikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi aṅkē pūcciyarippu, maṭkutal ēṟpaṭāvakiyil taḷattiṉmēl uyarttiyum puṉku vēmpu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ taḻikaḷip parappiyum, pātukāppar. avvāṇṭu muḻumikkum payaṉpaṭum. aṭutta āṇṭu viḷivu kuṟintālum kaḷañciyap poruḷ kavaliyiṉṟi vāḻa utavum.
ēruḻavar pōlac collēr uḻavarām cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ, akkālattārkkē aṉṟi ekkālattārkkum payaṉpaṭum vakiyil pātukāttup payaṉ koḷḷac ceyvatu nūl kaḷañciyamākum. 
intiyap parappil tamiḻ moḻiyil tāṉ mutaṉmutal `kalik kaḷañciyam uruvāṉatu eṉpatu perumitappaṭattakkatām. ippoḻutu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattāl veḷiyiṭappaṭum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam cevviyal moḻik koṭiyāka vantu, tamiḻmaṇ patippaka veḷiyīṭṭāl, tamiḻkūṟu nallulakap poruḷāvatu, cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tirumiku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷ tūṇṭal vaḻiyākat tulaṅkiya tulakkamākum. ātalāl paṭippāḷaṉ eṉṟa niliyil neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



tamiḻk kaḷañciyam
moḻiyiṉ vaḷam, ammoḻiyip pēcuvōr payaṉpaṭuttum coṟkaḷālum, am moḻiyil uḷḷa ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya vakikaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻiṉ vaḷam ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷilum niṟintuḷḷatu. coṟkaḷiṉ vaḷamum, perukkamum moḻiyiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭum. coṟkaḷiṉ perukkik kāṭṭa ettaṉiyō nikaṇṭukaḷum, akarātikaḷum, kaḷañciyaṅkaḷum tamiḻil vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ pala piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum tamiḻc coṟkaḷāyk karutip poruḷ tantuḷḷaṉa. piṟa moḻikaḷil ēṟiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷum vēṟṟāṭi uṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi aṟintu tamiḻeṉak kāṭṭa, moḻi uṇarvum, ilakkiya ilakkaṇap pulamiyum, tamiḻ muḻutaṟi takutiyum, makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷi vaḻaṅkum cūḻalil kēṭṭaṟitalum, coṟ poruḷāyvum, colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷum, vērc collāyvum, kūr matiyum niṟintirukka vēṇṭum.
ittamiḻc coṟ kaḷañciyam coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi maṭṭumā tarukiṉṟaṉa? coṟkaḷiṉ vērum, ataṉ virivum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkamum, cāṉṟukaḷum, makkaḷiṉ potu vaḻakkum, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkum, yāḻppāṇat tamiḻ vaḻakkum ceṟintuḷḷa tamiḻk kaḷañciyamām.
oru viṉic coṟkaḷ toki tokiyākiya toki ikkaḷañciyam. `aṭittal collaṭiyāka 145 coṟkaḷ uṇṇal vakikaḷ, ūrp peyar īṟukaḷ (504) nōy vakikaḷ (229), nōy viṉikaḷ (216), matil poṟi vakikaḷ (28) mali vakikaḷ (25) mutaliya ettaṉiyō vakikaḷum tokikaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ tokuppu kaṟpāri viyakka vikkum!
ik kaḷañciyat tokutikaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkaṇakkil maraput toṭarkaḷ, iṇi moḻikaḷ, olikkuṟippukaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭaṅki yuḷḷaṉa.
coṟkaḷ viḷakkattiṟku aṉṟāṭac ceytikaḷum (ceytittāḷkaḷ) cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkiya vakikaḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟu eṉpar. āṉāl, paṟpala nūṟuvakikaḷ uḷḷamiyi ikkaḷañciyam kāṭṭum.
mēṉāṭṭu uṭikaḷi māṭṭiyatāl tamiḻar mēṉāṭṭār ākāraṉṟō? pajji ētō piṟamoḻi eṉṟē mayaṅki niṟpar. atu paccik kāykaṟikaḷāl ceyyappaṭuvatu.
tēvanēyap pāvāṇar uṭaṉ paḻakiyamiyālum, avar nūlkaḷit tōyntu tōyntu kaṟṟamiyālum, avarē pārāṭṭiyamiyālum aiyā, pala coṟkaḷukku vērccol vaḻi mūlamum kiḷiyum kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. puḻuti, pūḻti, pūti.
pala coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: virippu- virippu; viṟippu - toyviṉmi;
ikkaḷañciyam tavacaṅkaḷām coṟkaḷ koṭṭappaṭṭu niṟintuḷḷatu. paṭippār tattamakkut tēviyāṉa tavacaṅkaḷi aḷḷi aḷḷip payaṉ koḷḷalām. evvaḷavu aḷḷiṉum eṉṟum kuṟiyātu ikkaḷañciyam.
tamiḻariṉ, moḻi, iṉa, ilakkiya, ilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ aṭaṅkalkaḷip paṭi paṭiyākat tiraṭṭit tokuttu veḷiyiṭum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil mutaliṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
aiyā mutumuṉivar, iḷami mutal tokutta coṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷic cērttuc cērmāṉamākkit tiraṭṭit tantuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷē! iṅkē vammiṉ! kaḷañciyam koṇmiṉ!! tamiḻi vaḷarmiṉ!!!
pi. tamiḻakaṉ.

aṇinturi
centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉṉum innūl iṉṟu nammiṭiyē vāḻum tolkāppiyarāka viḷaṅkum mūtta tamiḻaṟiñar centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa oṉṟākum. ēṟattāḻa 8000 tamiḻc coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ viḷakkattiṉi mēṟkōḷ cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum innūl tōrāyamāka 3254 pakkaṅkaḷil pattut tokutikaḷāka veḷivantu taṉṉērilāta tamiḻ moḻikkut taṉipperumi cērkkum muṉṉōṭip paṇiyāka amintuḷḷatu. akaramutalikaḷil iṭampeṟāta coṟkaḷ, marapuccoṟkaḷ, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ pōṉṟavi ikkaḷañciyattil iṭampeṟuvatu kuṟippiṭattakka tākum. ici, maruttuvam, kalveṭṭu, aṟiviyal ākiya tuṟikaḷ cārnta iṉṟiyamiyāta  coṟkaḷukkum poruḷ viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akarattil toṭaṅkum coṟkaḷuk kāṉa poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē 276 pakkaṅkaḷil mutal tokutiyākavum taṉit tokutiyākavum amintiruppatu nūliṉ ceṟiviyum viriviyum paṟi cāṟṟuvatāka amintuḷḷatu. ayaṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ aṟavē tavirkkap paṭṭiruppatu nūliṉ taṉicciṟappākum.
coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyap paṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil nūlāciriyarukkut tūṇṭutalāka amintatu olkāp pukaḻ tolkāppiyamē. kuṟippāka, ellāc collum poruḷkuṟittaṉavē (tol. 640) eṉṉum nūṟpā. in nūṟpāviṉi mūla muḻakkamākak koṇṭatu tamiḻmoḻi eṉpati viḷakkum poruṭṭē ikkaḷañciyam uruvākkap paṭṭateṉa nūlāciyir kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar kāṭṭiyavāṟu āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉak kāṇāmal veḷippaṭiyāka aṟiyakkūṭiya coṟkaḷukku maṭṭumē amitiyum tiripum poruḷ viḷakkamum kāṭṭuvatu ikkaḷañciyam eṉpatum nūlāciriyar kūṟṟākum. poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉūṭē kīḻkkāṇum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭiṉiyum uṇaralām.
poruṇmi teritalum coṉmi teritalum
colliṉ ākum eṉmaṉār pulavar.	(tol. 641)
teripuvēṟu niliyalum kuṟippiṉ tōṉṟalum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa poruṇmi niliyē. 	(tol. 642)
poruṭkup poruḷteriyiṉ atuvaram piṉṟē.	(tol. 874)
poruṭkut tiripilli uṇartta valliṉ	(tol. 875)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.	(tol. 877)
oru poruḷ pala coṟkaḷ mikavum nuṭpamāy ārāyap paṭṭut tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. `aḻaku poruṇmiyik kuṟikkum 67 taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷi aṭiyāḷaṅkaṇṭu . nuṭpamāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷit tantiruppatu āciriyariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻapulattiṟkum vaḷamāṉa paṭṭaṟiviṟkum cāṉṟā kaviḷaṅkukiṟatu.
perumatippiṟkuriya aiyā iḷaṅkumarāṉāriṉ neṭuṅkāla avā innūl veḷiyīṭṭiṉ vaḻi niṟivēṟiyuḷḷami makiḻvaḷikkiṟatu. itu kālatti veṉṟuniṟkum nilitta paṇi. tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa taliciṟanta nōkkīṭṭu nūl. ulakeṅkum vāḻum tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷ innūliṉi varavēṟṟup pōṟṟuvārkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. kaḷañciyap paṇi oru toṭar paṇi. ippattut tokutikaḷi aṭittaḷamākak koṇṭu toṭarntu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tokutikaḷ veḷivantu tamiḻiṟku vaḷamcērkka vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ avā.
taṉittamiḻp paṟṟāḷarākavum ariya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ patippuc cemmalākavum viḷaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar tiru. kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār ikkaḷañciyatti arumuyaṟciyuṭaṉ ciṟappuṟa veḷikkoṇarntiruppatu pārāṭṭiṟkuriyatākum.
muṉivar ka. irāmacāmi
muṉṉāḷ pērāciriyar - tuṇi iyakkunar
intiya moḻikaḷiṉ naṭuvaṇ niṟuvaṉam, micūr
muṉṉāḷ poṟuppu aluvalar
cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉam, ceṉṉi

nuḻivuri
tamiḻ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil putintu kiṭakkum aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit taṉakkē uriya naṭiyil iruntu  tamiḻiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷi akaḻntu kāṭṭum ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ niṉivāṟṟali niṉintu niṉintu  makiḻpavaṉ.
avar arukil irukkavum, avaruṭaṉ uriyāṭavum, vāḻnāḷil eṉakkuk kiṭitta bparumnpaṟākavumfrutukiṟēṉ.bkhÊPhÆW pāvāṇar, centamiḻ aṟiñar ilakkuvaṉār,mūtaṟiña®cemmašva.cupa. māṇikkaṉār mutalāṉa perumakkaḷi eṭṭi iruntu pārttavaṉ. avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu nalaṉkaḷik paṭittuc cuvittavaṉ. ip perumakkaḷiṉ motta uruvamāka ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉārip pārttu neñcam nekiḻpavaṉ.
ayyā avarkaḷiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷik kaṭanta kālattil pēraḷavil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu avariṉ uḻippi tamiḻkūṟum ulakukku aḷittatil niṟivu aṭikiṟēṉ.
iṉṟu uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam eṉum ivvaruntamiḻt tokuppi uruvākkuvataṟku evvaḷavu kālam uḻittiruppār eṉpati niṉittup pārkkiṟēṉ. viyantu pōkiṟēṉ. iccentamiḻt tokuppu tamiḻ akarāti varalāṟṟil tamiḻukkup putu varavākum. ituvari yārum ceyya muṉvarāta perumpaṇi yākum. ayyāviṉ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇap perumparappit tāṅkivarum ippoṟ kuviyali ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumakiḻcci aṭikiṟēṉ.
 pāvāṇar pōl vērkāṭṭi, vaḷam koṭukkum coṟkaṭalāy vāḻum ayyāviṉ pēruḻippāl viḻinta ilakkiya ilakkaṇak kaḷañciyatti veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam eṉṉiyē nāṉ perumip paṭuttik koḷkiṟēṉ. eṉ vāḻkkiyil kiṭitta perum pēṟākak karutukiṟēṉ. 
ayyāviṉ 75ām (30.01.2005) akavi niṟivi tiruccit tirunakaril tamiḻar aṉivarum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ koṇṭāṭiya nikaḻvil pulamikkut talivaṇakkam eṉum niṟivumalariṉi tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu makiḻntatu. anta malaril malēciyap pāvaracu cu.ai.ulakanātaṉ eḻutiya pāṭaliṉi kīḻē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ.
vāyttirukkum akaviyelām
     varalāṟu paṭikkiṉṟār
      ivayakamētntunpāṟṟue« muṉ cāṉṟāka vāḻum `aiyā avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa ikkaḷañciyatti tamiḻ ulakukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumiyum, pūrippum aṭikiṟēṉ.

āciriyar paṇi : 
toṭakkap paḷḷi - 16ām akaviyil toṭakkam
uyarnilip paḷḷi - tamiḻāciriyappaṇi
āyvuppaṇi : 
pāvāṇaruṭaṉ - centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭam, maturik kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakattil - kalic col viḷakkam tokuppup paṇi, tamiḻaka vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc col tokuppup paṇi.
tamiḻppaṇi : 
kaṭṭuri, kaviti, kati, nāṭakam, kāppiyam, ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya uri - tamiḻc collāyvu - paḻantamiḻnūl patippukaḷ - taṉimaṉita - ilakkaṇa - iyakka, ici, varalāṟu.  tamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam - nāṭakam.  kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ - āyvup poḻivu - tokuppu nūlkaḷ nāḷoṉṟukku 18 maṇinēra eḻuttup paṇi tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvi iyakkutal.  centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam ...
nūlkaḷ : 
civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi 420kkum mēl
veḷināṭṭu payaṇam : 
tamiḻīḻam, ciṅkappūr, malēyā, amerikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tamiḻ amippukaḷil poḻivukaḷ.
tirumaṇam nikaḻttutal : 
tamiḻakam, ciṅkappūr, malēyāvil 4000kkum mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻneṟit tirumaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻttunar.
iyakkappaṇi : 
tamiḻakat tamiḻāciriyar kaḻakam, ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakam, tirukkuṟaḷ pēravi, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tamiḻccāṉṟōr pēravi
virutu : 
ilakkiyac cemmal, centamiḻ antaṇar, mutumuṉivar mutaliyaṉa.

patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ		:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr		:	uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷit 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimik kaḻakam eṉṉum amippiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻittavar. iḷam talimuṟikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkiyi neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭa maṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakatti niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyum, putiya paṭippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumiyākap poruḷ vaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttiri patittu varupavar.
potunili
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tanti periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōri vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkikaḷi niṟivēṟṟa   ayarātu uḻittu varupavar.

ukir:
ukir = nakam. nakam, paṟavikkum vilaṅkukkum naṇṭu mutalām ūrikaḷukkum uḷḷa uṟuppām. paṟavi vilaṅku āyavaṟṟiṉ naka amippu. avaṟṟiṉ ceyal eṉpavaṟṟi aṟintāltāṉ, appeyarīṭṭiṉ arumi pulappaṭum.
avvukiriṉ kūrmi, ceḻumi āyavaṉṟi vaḷivum neṭumiyum koṇṭamiyāl uṇavip paṟṟavum pakiyi vellavum tuṇiyākiṉṟatu. makaḷiriṉ ukir kīri vakiyi eḷimiyāyk kiḷḷi eṭukkap payaṉpaṭutal kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kuruti oḻukavum uyir oḻiyavum kīri mutaliya koyyavum payaṉpaṭum ukir. acceyalkaḷik koṇṭē peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kuruti uka uyir uka (kuruti oḻuka) eṉpaṉa eṇṇat takkavi.
uku + ir = ukir. ukutal eṉpataṟku utiral, aḻalal, cintal, corital, poṭiyātal, viṭutal mutalām poruḷ kaḷit tarutali akara mutalikaḷ kuṟikkum. ukukka vikkum uṟuppiṉ peyar ukir eṉṟāyatu.

ukkāri:
uru > ukku + ār + i = ukkāri; atu piṭṭu.
piṭṭu - piṭittu vikkappaṭṭa māvu; ular īrappatam uṭiyatu; pola pola eṉa utirvatu. ātalāl ukkāri eṉappaṭṭatu. ukutal = oḻukutal; utirtal, pitirtal (tivā).
ukkāri:
iṉippu eṉṉum poruḷil ukkāri eṉpatu. pārppaṉar vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu.akkāra aṭicil eṉpatu kaṟkaṇṭu mutaliya iṉippuc cōṟu. u eṉpatu uyarvup poruḷ tarutalāl uyarnta - viruppuṭiya - iṉippuṇavu kuṟittu ukkāri eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ukkāri - ukkāri (ta. co. a).
ukkutal:

urumuraṟ ṟaṉṉa uṭkuvaru muracam	-puṟam.197
piḷḷi etik kaṇṭatō? eṉṉavō? ukkip pōyviṭṭatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
ucattu:
uyarttu eṉpatu pōliyāy(ya-ca) ucattu eṉappaṭum. ayarcci - acatti eṉappaṭuvatu pōla. ucattu eṉpatu uyara mākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa aṇiyik kuṟippatu cēraṉmātēvi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum ucatti eṉpatu uyartti eṉṉum poruḷil varutal potu vaḻakkākum.
ucāttuṇi:
ucāvutal = kalanturiyāṭal, uṇmi kāṇpataṟku ārāytal,vaḻakkil cāṉṟukaḷi viṉāvutal eṉa innāḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṟkeṉa aminta ūrp potu iṭam cāvaṭi atu, ucāvaṭi ukaram keṭṭuc cāvaṭi eṉa niṉṟatu. paḻanāḷil talivikku aminta tōḻikaḷ iruvakiyar. oruvakiyar ucāttuṇi; maṟṟoruvar acāttuṇi.
ucāttuṇi eṉpār talivikku nalamāṉavaṟṟi eṇṇi viṉāvi ēṟṟavi ceyyum tuṇi. taliviyiṉ ayarvu maṉakkavali pōkkuvār acāttuṇi.

poruḷ: neṭiya vaḻiyik kaṭantu virikiṉṟa kutirikaḷ taḷara vēṇṭāmalē niṉ karutteṉṉām eṉa viṉāvuvārip peṟṟāl naṉṟām ucāvuvār = karuttaṟivār.
nalaṅkēḻ arivi pulappu acā viṭavē	-kuṟun. 338

eṉṟum varum uvamikaḷāl aṟiyalām.
ucāvaṭi:
ucāvu + aṭi = ucāvaṭi = ūr vicāraṇi nikaḻum iṭam.
araciyal atikārikaḷ āṇṭutōṟum talimiyūr kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu taṅkiyiruntu appakuti ūrkaḷip paṟṟiya aṉittu āyvukaḷiyum nikaḻttum potu iṭam. aracu vari vāṅkum niliyamumākum.
cātivari iṉavari veṭṭivari maṟṟum ucāvaṭiyil kaṭṭum varikaḷum (ka.ka.a.mu 65)
ucuppal:
ucuppal = eḻuppal, ēviviṭal. nāyi uc uc eṉak kūppiṭal uṇṭu. uccuk kāṭṭal, ūccuk kāṭṭal - aḻittal; atu paṭuttiruntāl eḻupputalum, oṉṟaṉ mēl ēvutalum ucukkāṭṭal eṉa vaḻaṅkum .avvaḻakkattiliruntu uṟaṅkupavari eḻupputal ucuppal eṉa āyiṟṟu. eppaṭi ucuppiyum eḻumpa villi eṉpatu kumpakaṉṉa uṟakkattāri eḻuppuvār kuṟi kūṟal. ucumpal = eḻumputal. aṇilip piṭikka nāyi ucukkāṭṭuvār mikappalar.
ucupputal:
uyirpputal > ucupputal = uyirpōṉatu pōl kiṭappavaṉi- kiṭappati - eḻuppi viṭṭu uyirt tuṭippuṭimi yākkuvatu.
	* ucuppal kāṇka.
ucumpal:
ucumpal = eḻumpal.
atirppiṉ ucumpa	-peruṅ. 3:13:59
kuḻanti ucumpiviṭṭatu ōṭip pōyt  tūkku eṉpatu vaḻakku.


uccantali:
uccam + tali = uccantali; uccam > ucci, tali ucci, uccantali eṉappaṭṭatu. ilakkaṇap pōli.
uccantaliyil veyil aṭikkiṟatu; tuṇṭip pōṭṭuk koḷ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. uccikkiḷi, ucci mēṭu, uccip piḷḷiyār eṉa uccam ucciyākavum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. ucciyil viḷakkeṇṇey vittut tēy; cūṭu kuṟiyum  eṉpatum vaḻakku.
ucci eṭuttal, vakiṭu eṭuttalām. ucciṉāl pōl eṟi eṉṟāl taliyil  paṭumāṟu kuṟipārttu eṟi eṉpatām. piṉṉark kūrmiyāy uriya iṭam pārttu eṟi eṉṟāyatu.
uccam:
uccu > uccam. ukaraccuṭṭu, uyaraccuṭṭumām.
mikak kūṭutal. veyil iṉṟikku uccamāka aṭikkiṟatu; kuṭiyum ceruppu millāmal veḷiyē talikāṭṭa muṭiyātu eṉpatu vaḻakku. viyāḻaṉ uccattil irukkiṟāṉ ippoḻutu  eṉpatu kaṇiyam.
* umpar kāṇka.

uccukkāṭṭal:
paṭuttirukkum nāyi eḻuppi viṭac ceyyum olikkuṟippu. uc uc eṉṟatum nāy eḻuntu muṉṉē niṟpati veruṭṭum. ucupputal = eḻupputal. ucuppuk kāṭṭutal > uccuk kāṭṭutal.
* ucuppal  kāṇka.
uccuk koṭṭal:
uccuk koṭṭal:1
kēṭṭal, oppuk koḷḷal, varuttam terivittal. uc uc eṉpatu vāyiṉ olikkuṟippu. oruvar varuntak takka allatu uṇarvūṭṭat takka oru ceytiyic collum  pōtu ataṉik kēṭpavar vāyāl ucciṭṭuk koṇṭu kēṭpatu vaḻakkam. avvāṟu kēṭpati uccuk koṭṭal eṉpar.
uccuk koṭṭutal eṉpatu ēṟṟuk koḷḷutal, oppuk koḷḷutal, varuttam terivittal mutaliya poruḷkaḷit taruvatāy vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. collum pōtu uccu uccu eṉṟu kēṭṭu viṭṭu ippoḻutu colvatip pārēṉ; ceyvatip pārēṉ eṉṟu māṟupaṭa naṭakkum pōtu iṭittuk kāṭṭuvatum vaḻakkē.
* ūm pōṭal  kāṇka.
uccuk koṭṭal:2
cuviyāṉa paṇṭam tiṉṟavar ataṉi mēlum virumpik kēṭṭāl,  eṉṉa uccuk koṭṭiṉālum iṉi illi ; tīrntu pōyviṭṭatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
uccutal:

ucci:

uṭampaṭikki:
iruvarum ottu eḻutum uṟutippāṭṭu eḻuttu uṭampaṭikki eṉappaṭum. āḷpavar varalāṟṟil avarkaḷ ceyta uṭampaṭikkic ceytikaḷ uṇṭu.
* uṭampāṭu kāṇka.
uṭampaṭumey:
uṭam(ṉ) + paṭu + mey = uṭampaṭumey.
uyirīṟṟuc colliṉ muṉ uyir mutal col varumāṉāl avviṭattu iraṇṭu uyirkaḷukkum iṭiyē. avvuyirkaḷi uṭampaṭuttuvataṟku - iṇippataṟku - oru  mey yeḻuttu varum. ammeyyeḻuttiṟku uṭampaṭu mey eṉpatu peyar. īruyirkaḷ iṇital illi; iṇikka  varum mey (uṭal) eḻuttu uṭampaṭu meyyāyiṟṟu. avvāṟu varum eḻuttukaḷ y, v eṉpavi.
i ī ai vaḻi yavvum ēṉi
uyirvaḻi vavvum ēmuṉ irumiyum
uyirvariṉ uṭampaṭu meyyeṉ ṟākum	-naṉ.162
yakara uṭampaṭumey.
i. maṇi + aṭittāṉ = maṇiyaṭittāṉ
ī. tēṉī + eṅkē = tēṉīyeṅkē
ai. pāli + aruntu = pāliyaruntu
vakara uṭampaṭumey.
a. maṇa + aṭi = maṇavaṭi.	
ā. palā + atu = palāvatu.
u. vaḻu + illi = vaḻuvilli.	
ū. pū + eṅkē = pūveṅkē.
e. e + ili = evvili.		
o. no + arumi =  novvarumi.
ō. nō + illi =  nōvilli  
au. ku + aḻakitu  kuvaḻakitu.
ēmuṉ irumi ( ya, va)

19ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ō eṉpatum ē yip pōl irumi uṭampaṭu meykaḷiyum ēṟkum vaḻakkam pukuntatu.

kō + il = kōyil; kō + il = kōvil. āṉāl paḻantamiḻāṭci kōyil eṉpatē,
illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki yākum 	-tol. 293
uṭampāṭu:
uṭam(ṉ) + pāṭu = uṭampāṭu.
uṭaṉpaṭṭu - oṉṟupaṭṭu- ēṟṟal; uṭaṉpāṭu > uṭampāṭu ākum.
uṭampāṭil lātavar  vāḻkki kuṭaṅkaruḷ
pāmpō ṭuṭaṉuṟin taṟṟu	-tiruk. 890
ōr aracukkum maṟṟōr aracukkum oppukiyāṉa ceytiyi allatu tiṭṭatti eḻuti irupakkattavarum oppamiṭutal uṭampaṭikki ākum. īrūriṉarkkum īruṟaviṉarkkum ittakiya uṭampaṭikkikaḷ eḻutap paṭuvatum uṇṭu.
varalāṟṟut tuṟiyil uṭampaṭikki eḻututalum ataṉi mīṟalum pakittalum pōriṭalum mīḷa uṭampaṭikki eḻututalum kāṇalām.
uṭampiṭi:
uṭaṉpiṭi > uṭampiṭi.
vēliṉ oruvakiyāṉa muttali vēl mukkūṟupaṭṭa atu tākkuṅkāl mukkūṟum oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟu iṇintu mūvēl muṉiyum oruṅkē tākkalāl uṭampiṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
uṭampu: 
uṭal.
* uṭal kāṇka.
uṭal:
uṭal:1
uṭal = vippu mutal.
uṭampu, yākki, mey mutalām peyarkaḷiṭiya uṭal mutalāku peyarāka niṟkum. uyir uṭalōṭu irukkum kālamē vāḻvuk kālamākak koḷḷappaṭum. vāḻ mutalāvatu atu. emmutalukkum mutalāvatu uṭal mutalēyām. mutalik koṇṭē, vēṇṭum mutalit tēṭalām; mutaṉmiyum uṟalām; mutalvaṉiyum aṭiyalām; mutalvaṉum ākalām.
kalveṭṭu vaḻiyē vippu mutalākavum aṟiya varukiṉṟatu. aṟac ceyalkaḷukku vippu niti vittu ataṉ vaṭṭiyi maṭṭum eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam paṇṭē iruntatu. avvippu nitiyākiya mutal uṭal eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
tiruvāparaṇam tiruppaṇikku uṭalāyc celvatāka mutal kuṭutta kācu irupatu
uṭalkoṇṭu pūciyum tiruppaṇiyum celuttu vatākavum
uṭal kōḷukku iṉṉāyaṉārkkut tēva tāṉamāka viṭṭanilam (ka.ka.a.mu 66)
uṭal:2
uṭal = uṭampu.
uḻalutalukku eppoḻutum iṭamāka iruppatāl uṭal eṉappaṭṭatu eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭa karuttu. uḻalutal > uṭalutal > uṭal eṉpatu vaḻipporuḷ.
iṭumpikkē koḷkalam kollō eṉa viṉāvi illi eṉṟatu vaḷḷuvam(1029). uṭaṟṟutal eṉpataṟku aḻittal, ulittal, citaṟa aṭittal, ciṉattal, turattal, pakittal, porutal, varuttutal mutalām poruḷkaḷi akara mutalikaḷ tarukiṉṟaṉa.
uṭaṟkuṟiyākak  kuṟaḷ, ceviṭu, mūṅki, kūṉ, kuruṭu, maruḷ, uṟuppili eṉavum, uṭaṟkuṟṟamākap paci,nīr vēṭṭal, accam, vekuḷi, uvaki, vēṇṭal, niṉippu, uṟakkam, nari, nōy, iṟappu, piṟappu, matam, iṉpam, viyappu, viyarppu, kiyaṟavu eṉavum kūṟuvar. mēlum taliyil coṇṭu, coṟi, īr, pēṉ, coṭṭi, vaḻukki; mūkkil caḷi; vāyil umiḻnīr; kātil kuṟumpi; kaṇṇil pīḻi, viyarvi, nīr, malam eṉavum kūṟi ivvuṭal niliyaṟṟatu poyyāṉatu aḻivatu eṉa veṟuppar itaṟku iṭiyē ivvuṭal mey eṉavum, ivvuṭaṟ poṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyētāṉ aiyaṟivum āṟām aṟivum teyvap pēṟum peṟa vēṇṭum eṉavum ivvūṉuṭal ālayam eṉavum kūṟiya meyyuṇarvāḷarkaḷum iruntaṉar. poyt tuṟavar pārvikkum meyttuṟavar pārvikkum uḷḷa iṭiveḷi malikkum maṭuvukkukkum uḷḷa iṭiveḷi pōṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
uṭalip pōl uyarpaṭippu oṉṟilli eṉpatu cuṭṭik kāṭṭa vēṟoṉṟu vēṇṭuvatu illi. ciṟṟōṭṭi paṭṭāl pantu paṭuttu viṭukiṟatu! untukaḷil tēyvi (ṭayar) uḷavē! avaṟṟiṉ uḷḷē iruppatu. kāṟṟuttāṉē! akkāṟṟu ettaṉi kal(ṭaṉ) cumi tāṅkukiṟatu. tēyviyil ciṟu tuḷi viḻuntu viṭṭāl? āṉāl kaṇṇukkut teriyum ōṭṭi oṉpatu iruntum uyir ulāvum kālamellām ulāvik koṇṭuḷatē! uṟaṅkum pōtum ulāvukiṟatē! ovvōr aṇuviṉum ulāvukiṟatē! kuṭaliyakkam kurutiyiyakkam mūcciyakkam uṭaliyakkam ellām tāmāka iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉavē!
itaṉ mūlam, oru paṉittuḷi aḷaviṉum ciṟiya vintu tāṉē! ataṉ viḷivu ettakiya vinti - viyappu - ākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl ivvuṭal uyiruṭaṉ nalamāka nīṭac ceytal kaṭami eṉpatu kāṭṭa clš> uṭam > uṭampu > uṭami eṉak karuti vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatālēyē, uṭali mutal eṉṟaṉar ilakkaṇar. mutalāku peyar, ciṉiyākupeyar eṉpavi aṟiyiṉ vāḻ mutal eṉpatu uṭalē eṉpatu pulappaṭum! vāḻ mutalām iṟi kōyil koṇṭa iṭam vāḻ mutalākattāṉē irukka vēṇṭum itaṉāltāṉē meyyuṇarvil mēmpaṭṭa tirumūlar.
uṭampiṉi muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampi ṉuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampi ṉuḷḷē uttamaṉ kōyil koṇṭāṉ eṉṟu
uṭampiṉi yāṉirun tōmpukiṉ ṟēṉē	-tiruman. 725
eṉṟārām.
uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar
tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉam cēravu māṭṭār	-tiruman. 724
eṉṟum avartāmē kūṟiṉār.
uṭalutal - uṭaṟṟutal - nōyum tīmiyum pakiyum piṟavum uṭaṟṟa eṉpati viṭuttu, avaṟṟi uṭaṟṟa - aḻikka - vāyttatu uṭalē eṉak kāṇal, uṭal uyir ulaku ākiya evaṟṟukkum vēṟum perumiyum āvatām. mutal ilārkku ūtiyam illi (tiruk. 449) eṉpatu ettaku tērcciyuri.
mutalām uṭal piṟappu mutal iṟappu vari mutalvaṉ vāḻviṭam eṉak koṇṭu vāḻvār vāḻvu mutalvaṉ vāḻvutāṉē! ammutalvaṉ vāḻvu vāḻ mutal vāḻvu eṉa uṭalōmpal uyirōmpal ulakōmpal koḷvōm! uṭampip peṟṟa payaṉ atuvē. uṭal aḻakā eṉiṉ, poyyuṭal pukaḻuṭal eṉa vakutta nuṇṇiyar nōkku eṉṉa, aḻiyum uṭalāl aḻiyā uṭalip peṟutal pōla arumi mutal ētāvatu uṇṭā? iṭṭa vittuttāṉē viḷivām!
poyyāyp pōvatām uṭal eṉṟum meyyāy irukkum uṭalit tarukiṉṟatu eṉṟāl poyyuṭalā itu! meyyuṭal tanta mey atu eṉpatu meyyallavā!
uṭal:3
tamiḻar kaṇṭa meyyiyal kōṭpāṭu oli eḻuttu eḻuppa - vari eḻuttu eḻuta-t toṭaṅkum pōtē eḻumpiya cāṉṟu, uyir eḻuttu  meyyeḻuttu mutalām, kuṟiyīṭu.  mey yeḻutti uṭal eṉṟaṉar. ēṉ?

uṭal paṟṟiya peyarkaḷ
uṭal (uṭaṉ): uyiruṭaṉ cērntē iyaṅkuvatu allatu uyiruṭaṉ iruppatu. uṭampum atu.
kūṭu: (kūṭu pōlvatu) kuñcu poritta piṉ toṭarpu nīṅkum muṭṭik kūṭu pōla uyiriviṭṭu nīṅkuvatu kuṭampi allatu kūṭu.  (tiruk. 338).
mey: (pōrvi, meymi). uyiri mēlākap potin tiruppatu; ōruyir iruppiyum iyakkattiyum oḻinta piṉṉarum meyppikkum vaṭivuṭiyatu. 
yākki: (kaṭṭappaṭṭatu). tōl narampu elumpu taci arattam mutaliya eḻuvakit tātukkaḷāl yākkap peṟṟiruppatu allatu muṭiyap paṭṭiruppatu yākki allatu muṭi.
kaṭṭi: (iyakkamaṟṟatu). uyir nīṅkiyapiṉ kaṭṭi pōlak kiṭantu erivatu allatu maṇṇōṭu maṇṇāyp pōvatu (tēva. 219).
uṭakku;(uṭal > uṭakku(taci) ).uḷḷīṭu aṟṟa uṭampuk kūṭu
mēṉi: (mēl > mēṉi) uṭampiṉ mēṟpuṟam.
uṭaṟṟunar:
uṭaṉāvar naṇpar; uṭaṟṟunar āvār pakivar. uṭaṟṟunar. uṭalunar eṉavum collappaṭuvār.

uytal yāvatu niṉ uṭaṟṟi yōrē								-patiṟ. 84
uṭaṉaṭi:
uṭaṉ + aṭi = uṭaṉaṭi(uṭaṉē)
vāṅkiya kaṭaṉi uṭaṉaṭiyāka (uṭaṉē) mīḷak kaṭṭiyāka vēṇṭum eṉṉum ikkaṭṭum ēṟpaṭum. vīṭṭuc celavum cari, nāṭṭuc celavum cari, uṭaṉaṭic celavu eṉpataṟku ōr irupput toki kaṭṭāyam vēṇṭum. kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷiṉ cāṉṟukaḷi uṭaṉaṭiyāka eṭukkāviṭṭāl maṟikkappaṭutal iyalpu.
uṭaṉaṭi eṉpatu nerukkaṭitāṉ! annerukkaṭiyum cila ākkaṅkaḷi ākkum. uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ eṉpatum itu.
uṭaṉili:
uṭaṉ + nili = uṭaṉili. uṭal nili eṉap pirippiṉ uṭal paṟṟiya niliyākiviṭum. atu nalam viṉātal.
uṭaṉili = oṉṟu paṭṭiruttal allatu kūṭiyiruttal.
uṭaṉili:1
ōriṭattu iruttal. cōḻaṉ kurāppaḷḷit tuñciya perun tirumāvaḷavaṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻutiyum oruṅkiruntārik kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār pāṭiyatu. tuṟi - uṭaṉili (puṟam. 58)
uṭaṉili:2
uḷḷattāl oṉṟupaṭṭiruttal.
oruvīr oruvīrk kāṟṟutir iruvīrum
uṭaṉili tiriyīr	-puṟam. 58
uṭaṉili meymmayakku:
uṭaṉ + nili + mey + mayakku = uṭaṉili meymmayakku.
kakaram mutal ṉakaram iṟutiyākiya patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷil ra, ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭu meykaḷ nīṅkiya patiṉāṟu tammuṭaṉ tām vantu mayaṅkum (kūṭi niṟkum allatu uṭaṉniṟkum). aḵtu uṭaṉili meymmayakkamām.
e-ṭu:
cukku, aṅṅaṉam, accam, maññi, paṭṭu, tiṇṇam, pattu, vennīr, appaṉ, ammi, meyyaṉ, alli, tevvar, paḷḷam, kāṟṟu, aṉṉi.
cukku eṉpatil uḷḷa k eṉṉum meyyi aṭuttuḷḷa ku eṉpatil k u eṉṉum eḻuttukaḷ uṭaṉili meym mayakku uṟṟiruttalik kāṇka. ivvāṟē piṟavumām.
uṭaṉuṟi:
uṭaṉ + uṟi = uṭaṉuṟi. oruvarōṭu oruvar oṉṟu kūṭi vāḻtal. oruvarōṭu oruvar eṉpatu talivaṉ taliviyarik kuṟittatām. ciṟappu vakiyil piṟarik kuṟippatāyiṉum potuvakiyil kuṟippatu talivaṉ taliviyariyē.
uṭaṉuṟi vāḻkkik kutavi yuṟiyum
kaṭavuḷarkaṇ taṅkiṉēṉ	-kali. 93
iṭiyil kāṭci niṉṉōṭu
uṭaṉuṟi vākkuka uyarntu pālē	-puṟam.236
uṭaṉkaṭaṉ:
muṉpiṉ illāmal kēṭṭavuṭaṉ koṭukkavum, koṭuttati mīḷak kēṭṭavuṭaṉ taruvatumākiya kaṭaṉ uṭaṉkaṭaṉām. ataṉi eḻuttāl varintu tarutal uṭaṉkaṭaṉ muṟi, uṭaṉkaṭaṉ ōli, uṭaṉkaṭaṉ varivu eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
ikkaṭaṉukku vaṭṭi uṇṭu. āṉāl, īṭu poruḷ illi. kiyeḻuttiṭalum cāṉṟiṭalumē kaṭaṉ tokikku īṭām. ataṉāl kaṭaṉ peṟṟavar celuttat tavaṟiṉālum celutta muṭiyā niliyil iruntālum cāṉṟoppam iṭṭārmēl naṭavaṭikki eṭukkappaṭuvatum, taṇṭamākac celuttuvatum naṭimuṟikkāṭci. ataṉāl, ēṟpatu ikaḻcci eṉpataṟku itaṉik kūṟuvārum uḷar.
piṇipaṭ ṭevarkkum piṉcol tītu
eṉpārum uḷar.
uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟal:
uṭaṉ + kaṭṭi + ēṟal. uṭaṉ = kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ.
kaṇavaṉ iṟantu cuṭaliyil kaṭṭimēl vikkap paṭṭāṉākiya avaṉoṭu avaṉ maṉivi uṭaṉ kaṭṭi  ēṟi  uyir oṭukkutal uṭaṉkaṭṭi  ēṟalām.
tīppāynta ammi 	eṉappaṭuvār ivar.
mikka eṉ kaṇavaṉ viṉippayaṉ uyppap
pukkuḻip pukuvēṉ eṉṟavaḷ pukutalum	-maṇimē. 16: 27-28
ivvaḻakkam āṅkilar āṭcik kālattil irācā rām mōkaṉar mutalōr muyaṟciyāl tiruvar (lord) peṇṭiṅku eṉpārāl caṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyē taṭukkappaṭṭatu.
uṭaṉkēṭaṉ:
uṭaṉkēṭaṉ:1
uṭaṉ + kēṭaṉ = uṭaṉkēṭaṉ. uṭaṉkēṭaṉ = kēṭuṟṟa pōtum uṭaṉiruntu tāṉum kēṭṭi aṭipavaṉ.
tāmum tammōṭu uṭaṉkēṭāṉa neñcamē yāyiruntatu nālā. 3946 uri.
uṭaṉkēṭaṉ:2
uṭaṉiruntu keṭuppavaṉum uṭaṉkēṭaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉ uṭpaki, iraṇṭakaṉ āvaṉ.
uṭaṉpaṭuttal:
uṭaṉpaṭuttal:1
uṭaṉpaṭuttal > uṭampaṭuttal. talivaṉ maṉiviyari oṉṟuvikkum pārvi uṭampaṭuttal eṉappaṭum (tol. 1042).
uṭaṉpaṭuttal:2
kātalaṉ kātaliyar, tam peṟṟōr tam kātali ēṟkāmal maṇattaṭi iṭuvar eṉiṉ, tām uṭaṉpōkku mēṟkoḷḷa nērum. ataṟkut talivaṉō taliviyō tayaṅkiṉ tōḻi irupālum uṭaṉpaṭutti uṭaṉpōkkuk koḷḷa vippāḷ. avaḷ ceyal uṭaṉpaṭuttal (uṭampaṭuttal) ākum. talivaṉ taliviyar koḷvatu uṭampaṭutal uṭampāṭu eṉappaṭum.
		* uṭaṉpōkku kāṇka.


uṭaṉpāṭu:
iruvar uṭaṉpaṭuvatum, palar uṭaṉpaṭuvatum, nāṭukaḷum pala nāṭukaḷum uṭaṉpaṭuvatum uṭaṉpāṭu eṉappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭṭi eḻutturuvākkuvatum uṭaṉpāṭu ākum. uṭaṉpaṭikki eṉpatum atu. uṭaṉpāṭu uṭampāṭu, uṭampiṭi eṉavum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu. 
uṭaṉpōkku:
talivaṉ taliviyar tam maṇattiṟkup peṟṟōr taṭiyāya vaḻit tāmē vēṟiṭam pōy maṇaṅkoḷvataṟkup pōtal uṭaṉpōkku (uṭampōkku) ākum.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉ pōtu maṟṟi ūrārāl maṇaccaṭaṅku nikaḻtalum, uṭaṉpōkku mīṭciyil tam kuṭumpattil maṇaccaṭaṅku nikaḻtalum paṇṭi vaḻakkamām.
uṭaṉpōkku vaki
pōkkaṟi vuṟuttal pōkkuṭaṉ paṭāmi
pōkkuṭaṉ paṭuttal pōkkuṭaṉ paṭutal
pōkkal vilakkal pukaḻtal tēṟṟal eṉṟu
yāppami uṭaṉpōk kirunāṉku vakittē	-nampi. 181
uṭaṉvayiṟṟōr:
oru tāy vayiṟṟil piṟantavar.
piṟappō raṉṉa uṭaṉvayiṟ ṟuḷḷum	-puṟam. 183
uṭaṉvayiṟ ṟōrkaḷ	-cilap. 10: 227
uṭaṉpiṟappueṉṟum,  uṭampiṟappu eṉṟum, piṟappu eṉṟum makkaḷ vaḻakkil curuṅkiṉum poruḷ atuvēyām. uṭaṉpiṟantār cuṟṟattār eṉṟirukka vēṇṭā eṉpati māṟṟi vāḻvārē - vāḻntu kāṭṭuvārē - uṭaṉpiṟappu eṉpataṉ cāṉṟāvār
iruvar kaḷappōril niṟkiṉṟaṉar. uṭaṉpiṟappi uṇartti neṟikāṭṭiya maṇ tamiḻmaṇ!
orukuṭaṟ paṭutara ōriri tuṟṟum
irutali orupuṟā
eṉṉum piṟappiṉar ippaṭi etiriṭi yākalāmā eṉak kēṭṭatu takaṭūr yāttiri. ataṉi iṉṟuvari kēṭṭatā tamiḻmaṇ?
uṭu:
uṭu:1
uṭu = uṭuttu eṉṉum ēval uṭuttal = cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal.
nīrārum kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭanti	-maṉōṉ. tamiḻ.
uṭu:2
viṇmīṇ. viṇmīṉiyum viṇmīṉ vaṭivākak kāṭṭum uṭukkuṟiyiyum kāṇiṉ vaṭṭamātal teḷivām.
uṭu:3
matilic cūḻa amintirukkum akaḻ. kōṭṭi vaṭṭamātal pōl ataṉ akaḻum vaṭṭamātalāl uṭu eṉappaṭṭatām.
uṭukkuṟi:
uṭu + kuṟi = uṭukkuṟi
uṭu = viṇmīṉ, kuṟi = aṭiyāḷam. viṭupāṭṭuk kuṟiyākavum, aṭiyāḷak kuṟiyākavum itu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu:

uṭukki:
uṭukki:1
uṭukki = uṭuppu; uṭuttiya uṭi.
uṭukki iḻantavaṉ kipōla	-tiruk. 788
uṭukki:2
icikkaruvi vakiyuḷ oṉṟāṉa tōṟ karuvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu uṭukki.
pēriki paṭakam iṭakki uṭukki
uṭukku eṉpatu itu. uṭukkaṭittal pēyōṭṭal iṉṟum oḻiyāmal ciṟṟūrkaḷil uṇṭu.
uṭuppu:
vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟik kaṭṭum uṭi uṭuppu āyiṟṟu. uṭu > uṭi. uṭu eṉṉum viṇmīṉ peyarum vaṭṭam kuṟittatē. vaṭṭuṭi eṉpatu pōr vīrar uṭuttiya uṭivakiyuḷ oṉṟām. uṭukki, uṭukku eṉpaṉavum vaṭṭam kuṟittaṉavē.
* kōṭaṅki kāṇka.
uṭumpu:
uṭumpu ūrvatām ōr uyiri. uṭu = vaṭṭam.
uṭumpup piṭi eṉa viṭāppiṭiyik kuṟippar. uṭumpiṉ vālip paṟṟi ataṉ vāyil tantuviṭṭal ataṉi viṭāmal paṟṟik koṇṭu paṟṟuk kuṟaṭupōl vaḷintu curuṇṭu kiṭakkum. uṭumpu vēṭṭiyar uṭumpip paṟṟi avvāṟu vālip paṟṟac ceytu vaḷiyaṅkaḷik kampil kōttuk koṇṭu varuvatupōl varutalik kaṇṭāl uṭumpiṉ iyalpum appeyariṭṭār aṟivuk kūrppum viḷakkamām.
uṭumpiyam eṉpatu perampalūr - āṟṟūrccāli vaḻiyil uḷḷatōr ūr. uṭumpukku iraṇṭu nāvuṇṭu eṉpar (ve.vi.pē). ataṉāl uṭuppu nākkaṉ eṉpatu poyyaṉi - iraṇṭakaṉi-k kuṟikkum eṉpatum atu. pāmpukkup piḷavu paṭṭa nākkuḷḷati niṉivu kūralām ataṉip piṇṇākku (piḷ nākku) eṉṟār kāḷamēkar(taṉip).
uṭi:
uṭi:1
uṭu + ai = uṭi cuṟṟik kaṭṭum āṭi . uṭu = vaṭṭam.
nīrārum kaṭal uṭuttalāl nilamakaḷ kaṭalāṭiyaḷ ākavilliyā? maravuri eṉpatu eṉṉa, marattiṟku uṭiyāka iruntatutāṉē!


uṭi:2
muḷ maravakikaḷuḷ uṭi maram eṉpatu oṉṟu; vaṟaṇṭa nilattu maram atu. oṭṭaṭi, uṭi, karuvēl,veḷvēl, caṭivēl eṉpavi avviṉattavi.
pāñcālaṅkuṟicci nāṭṭuppāṭalil , oṭṭaraṅkāṭām oṭaṅkāṭām eṉavarum.
uṭi:3
uṭikka eṉṉum ēval. tēṅkāy uṭi eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
uṭippil pōṭal:
taḷḷiviṭal. uṭippi aṭikka maṇṇiyum kalliyum pōṭuvar. cilar ericcalāl utavākkarip piḷḷikaḷiyum vēlikkārariyum uṉṉi uṭippil vikkalām, uṭippiltāṉ pōṭa vēṇṭum eṉpar. cila vēlikaḷic ceyyātu tavirttalik kiṭappil pōṭal eṉpatuṭaṉ uṭippil pōṭal eṉpatum uṇṭu. muṉṉatu paḷḷattil taḷḷal, piṉṉatu kālattit taḷḷal. uṭippil pōṭṭu mūṭappaṭṭatu veḷippaṭumā? putiporuḷ āyvārkku oru kālattup pulappaṭalām.
uṭimi:
uṭiyarām taṉmi, uṭimiyām. uṭi akalāmal cuṟṟiyum paṟṟiyum kākkappaṭuvatu pōlak kākkappaṭuvatu uṭimiyākum. ulaka uṭimiyām muḻumiyil iruntu uṭittu nām namakkeṉa eṭuttuk koḷḷum poruḷē uṭimiyām. parupporuḷām uṭimiyum, nuṇporuḷām uṭimiyum eṉa uṭimi iraṇṭām. nilapulam mutaliyaṉa muṉṉatu; aṉpu aṟivu mutaliyaṉa piṉṉatu.
uṭimiyuḷ oṉṟākap poruḷuṭimiyi vaḷḷuvar vittilar. pattu uṭimikaḷik kūṟiyavar, poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyip palapaṭa virittavar poruḷuṭimiyi uṭimiyākak kūṟāmi nōkkat takkatu.
aṟivuṭimi, aṉpuṭimi mutaliyavi tammiviṭṭu eṉṟum pōka viṭāmal vāḻum vāḻvē vāḻvām ! avvāṟu poruḷip pōkaviṭāmal vāḻum karumi vāḻvu vāḻvākātu! iraviṉum iḻivu akkaravu vāḻvu! eṉpatāl uṭimiyākak kūṟiṉār allar eṉka.
uṭimiyuḷ taliyāya uṭimi, uḷḷa muṭimi yākum.  uḷḷamuṭimi uṭimi (tiruk. 592); uṭiyar eṉappaṭuvatu ūkkam (tiruk. 591) eṉpavi avi.
uṭiyār:
uṭiyār:1
uṭimi + ār = uṭiyār = uṭimiyākak koṇṭavar
iṟivar, aracar, kuruvar, talivar mutalōrkkuriya virutup peyar. allatu matippuṟu peyar.
nammi uṭiya cakkaravartti srī irācarācatēvar,
uṭiyār srī irācarāca  tēvar,
srī irāca rājivaram uṭiyār
uṭiyār carva civapaṇṭita civāccāriyār (ka.ka.a.mu.26)

uṭkāyccal:

uṭkāyccal:2

uṭkārtal:
uṭkārtal:1
uṭku + ārtal = uṭkārtal.
uṭalukkum uḷḷuṟuppukaḷukkum uṇṭām uḷiccali ōyntu iruntu akaṟṟik koḷḷutal uṭkārtalām.
uṭkārtal:2
puṟattē pōkāmal vīṭṭiṉ uḷḷēyē - kucciliṉ uḷḷēyē iruttal. peṇkaḷ mutaṟ pūppaṭintu ataṉ caṭaṅku niṟivēṟum vari vīṭṭiṉpuṟattuk kuṭici yamittu uḷḷēyē irukkum nili uṭkārtalām. pūppu nīrāṭṭiṉ piṉ vīṭṭoṭu kūṭṭuvatu eṉpatu aṇmik kālam vari irunta ciṟṟūr vaḻakku. tāymāmaṉ muṟiyiṉarē akkuṭici  amippar. nīrāṭṭu muṭintapiṉ kuṭiciyi erittu viṭuvar.
uṭkāḻ:
uḷ + kāḻ = uṭkāḻ = uḷ vayiram.

akakkāḻ eṉpatum itu.
uṭkiṭi:

poruḷ: peritākiya tēṉiṟālik kaṇṭa muṟukiya kāl kaḷiyuṭiya naṭattalum niṟṟalumiṉṟi iruttaṟṟoḻiliyē yuṭiya muṭavaṉ, uḷḷaṅ kiyiṉic ciṟiya uṇkalamākak kuvittuk koṇṭu nilattē iruntapaṭiyē iruntu, attēṉi ṟālip paṉmuṟiyum ēṉik kiyāl cuṭṭikkāṭṭit taṉ vaṟuṅkiyi nakkiṉāl pōl(uri/ po. vē. cu.).
